[dzup-ui] DzButton: refused to render a navigation URL with scheme "javascript:" supplied on `href`. The attribute is omitted and the element renders as a non-link, carrying `data-state="url-rejected"`. Allowed by default: http, https, mailto, tel, sms, plus relative and fragment URLs. To widen or narrow this, set `url-policy` on DzProvider — there is no per-component opt-out, on purpose.
